focus = {
	id = KYO_make_sure_tengu_support
	icon = GFX_KYO_make_sure_tengu_support

	x = 2
	y = 1
	cost = 5

	search_filters = {
		FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL
		FOCUS_FILTER_INDUSTRY
	}

	prerequisite = {
		focus = KYO_make_sure_kappa_support
	}

	completion_reward = {
		KYO_cws_grant_tengu_focus_reward = yes

		set_global_flag = recruit_tenguclan

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KYO_player_civil_war_faction
				is_ai = no
			}
			set_country_flag = KYO_has_tengu_legacy_method
		}

		
		custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt

		set_technology = {

			KYO_tech_crow_tengu_support = 1

			KYO_tech_white_wolf_tengu = 1

			basic_small_airframe = 1
		}

		custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt

		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
			create_equipment_variant = {
				name = "Tengu Type-2 Gale"
				allow_without_tech = yes
				type = small_plane_airframe_1
				modules = {
					fixed_main_weapon_slot = light_mg_4x
					engine_type_slot = engine_1_2x
					special_type_slot_1 = air_air_radar_1
				}
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = small_plane_airframe_0
				variant_name = "Tengu Type-2 Gale"
				amount = 300
				producer = KYO
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = fighter_equipment_2
				amount = 300
				producer = KYO
			}
		}

		# 天狗情報與空運援助
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = KYO_tengu_orgnization_help
			days = 90
		}

		add_war_support = 0.025
		add_political_power = 50

		# 1民工
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				free_building_slots = {
					building = industrial_complex
					size > 0
					include_locked = yes
				}
			}

			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1

			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}

		# 1軍工
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				free_building_slots = {
					building = arms_factory
					size > 0
					include_locked = yes
				}
			}

			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1

			add_building_construction = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
}
